2a1(1): @##/ O /sman thams cad blangs nas ni//srang cig nges par bzhag par bya/
2a2(1): na mo rad+na tra ya s+ya//na mo badzra pA Ni s+ya/ba ti ba ra bA ti/ku shi sam ban na/mA na sA+e/
2a3(1): oM ta ra dA ba tes ye san tsA+e b+hu te/ad ta thA+e rU pI te/A smA mi swA hA//sku gdang ldan

2b4(1): de nas 'dod chags la sogs dang //sman mnyam par gzhog par 'gyur//nad dang sman kun ston pa dang //
3a1(1): @#/ O /gtam rgyud thams cad smra bar byed//sgrub pa po la legs nyes rnams//tshig gis

3b5(1): mchod rten gyi drung du//bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar mchod pa byas nas//sna ma'i me tog brgyad brgya la//lan brgyad brgya sngags

4a4(1): 'grub par 'gyur ro//de nas gang gi yang nyin par yang rung ste//cho ga de bzhin du sna ma'i brgyad brgya la lan brgyad brgya bzlas brjod byas te

4b2(1): e hi badzra pA NI/i da mA pA hA a nu sma shI g+h+ra ma na tsa pa la li ta ga ti/ma hA ya k+She sA+e ti badzra pA Ni/he he/hA+e hI hA+e hA+e/hi hi/he ku ru he ku ru//
4b3(1): ku ku/ku ku/hu badzra pA Ni/sa ra sa ra/bai ri bai ri/hA+e hI hA+e hi hA+e hI/ku ku b+ha/ku ku b+ha/badzra pA ni swA hA////'di ni snying po spyan 
4b4(1): drang ba'o//de la 'di ni lag ni rdo rje'i snying po yin no//sar+b+bad tra a pa ra tsi tan sar+b+bad tra a pra ti ha tan/sar+b+bad arta Ta sa naM/o dza ste dzo krA ma

5a1(1): @#/ O /puSh+Ti ka ran/ba sha ki ra naM/sa be no pa da/rnyan ba ra dan/sar+b+pa ka ma ar+tha sa d+ha kan/a kar Sha nan badzra NI

5a3(1): nya pa ya ti/badzra na/ha n+wa ti da ha ti/pa tsa ti/tshin+d+ha ti/bin+t+ra pa ya ti/A lA+o ga bi lo kang+ka rI ti/hi li ni li/mi li/o u ho b+ha 
5a4(1): badzra pA Ni/hu ru hu ru/san b+han+da/b+han+da /ye tu sh+ta stan ha na ha na/ha da ha da/pa tsa pa tsa/g+h+ri h+na gri h+na/bash+yang ka ro hi/ha la ha lA/
5a5(1): ya swA hA/hu la hu lA ya swA hA/hi li hi la yA swA hA/ku lu ku la ya swA hA/ki li ki la ya swA hA/ki le ki la yA hri da ya ma nu sman ra badzra pA Ni//
5a6(1): ma hA yak+Sha sA+e na pa ti sa ma ya/pra ti grI h+ya hu tin d+hu pa pu shA gan d+ha mA/l+yan tsa ra sra ma hu dra/sa ma yan bya ha kri ma mad ma sa ma yo bya d+hi Sh+ya ti ra badzra pA Ni/hi 
5b1(1): li ha ri ni/i li ku ru kun Tu pra sa shag to smin/tu ru me ba ra hi ri li/ni ri li/badzra pA Ni/ti li ti li/bi sha ya ma nu brta/hi ni ri/ni ri Ni badzra pA Ni /

5b3(1): hi li ti/li pi li ri/u b+ho u b+ha/hu bu hu ru/ban+da ban+da/ba sh+ye mye ka ro ha da na swAhA//lag na rdo rje la phyag 'tshal byas/rtse da mo can zhes 
5b4(1): bya ba'i snying po 'di na 'jig rten grags//de'i 'dod rgyu sgrub pa yin//gang zhig dngos grub mchog 'dod cing //'di'i sgrub pa'i don shes pa//des ni

6a3(1): dbus dag tu ni gzhag par bya//de yi steng du rdo rje de//de dag me tog 'phreng bas rgyan//rtag tu yang ni dus gsum du//mchod pa rnam pa sna tshogs 
6a4(1): bya|tsam pa ka dang //tsam pa ka'i//me tog sna ma'i me tog rnams//yid du 'ong bas sangs rgyas dang //rdo rje 'dzin la nges par mchod//
6a5(1): du ru ka dang a ka ru//gdug pa'i mchog gis rtag tu bsreg/sku gzugs chog bzhin bris la//cho ga 'di ni bya ba ste//sangs rgyas gser 
6a6(1): gyi kha dog 'dra//sangs rgyas thad na gnas pa yi//lag na rdo rje g.yon phyogs bri//rgyal po bzhin du rgyan bya zhing //ha cang dmar bar mi bya ste//
6a7(1): sku gzugs nub phyogs logs su bstan//shes rab can gyis mchog 'di bya ste//de ni rim pa bzhin du bshad//dang por yar gyis tshes cig 
6b1(1): brtsam//nges par gnas shing gtsang ba yis//khrus ni ba lang lci ba dang //ba gcin tshags pas dus gsum bya//mdun du rtag tu gos phrugs ni//bdug 
6b2(1): pa rab tu bdug pa'i bgo/tsam pa ka dang tsam pa ka'i//me tog rnams kyis rtag tu mchod//sna tshogs dri dang me tog dang //mdzes shing yid du 'ong
6b3(1): ba'i mchod//lan grangs brgyad brgyar bzlas brjod bya//du ru ka dang a ka ru//sreg cing mar me gnyis kyang dbul//rtag tu 'chi bar mi bya ste//
6b4(1): de bzhin mar gyi mar me gzhag/nyin gcig bzhin du bskyed par bya//de tshe 'o ma'i 'de gu rnams//zang zhing mnyam par gzhag pa dang //gso spyod

17a3(1): sha dzA/bid+h+ya pra yug ta//mu la karma su//pa da pang su/ra dzan tsA+e bi/sarba me tA ni ka ra yA+e da/e ka ud ta ba nA+e tA+o//de ne rI bar /b+hi nA sha nA+e//sar+b+ha kar+m+ma ka ri pad+ya ma

18b6(1): zhal nas A d+ha rnyil//yang na mthon ka chen po 'byung //'dod pa'i dbang phyug kha nas kyang //bzang po'i dkyil 'khor steng du ltung //lag na rdo rje'i kha nas kyang //
